## 九、董事会报告

#### 1、2015年总体经营情况回顾

2015年，本公司主要经营指标完成情况如下：

(1) 资产总额704.35亿元，比上年增加171.01亿元，增长32.06%；

(2) 各项存款519.86亿元，比上年增加123.36亿元，增长31.11%；

(3) 各项贷款356.99亿元，比上年增加67.72亿元，增长23.41%；

(4) 各项收入38.47亿元，比上年增收6.05亿元，增长18.66%；

(5) 实现净利润6.72亿元，比上年减少2.87亿元，减幅29.95%；

(6) 净资产收益率12.51%，比上年降低7.89个百分点；

(7) 不良贷款年末余额6.70亿元，比上年末增加了3.99亿元；占比1.88%，比上年末上升了0.94个百分点。

#### 2、2015年董事会工作回顾

##### (1) 切实保障了公司治理结构的稳定性

2015年，本公司董事会以“紧抓大事、明确方向、及时决策”为工作策略，密切结合实际，从制度建设、业务开拓、风险管控、机构设置及高管配备等多方面依照股东大会授予的权限充分发挥作用，全年共召开董事会会议16次，审议事项35项，听取报告6个。各专业委员会共召开会议25次，审议事项33项，听取报告1个，保障了本公司公司治理结构的稳定可靠运行。

##### (2) 有效发挥了业务指导作用

2015年初，面对极其困难的经营形势，董事会认真分析内外发展因素，坚持以改革创新推动业务发展的原则，实现持续经营能力的提高。全年，通过利率定价政策稳定线下融资保基础，通过市场化导向增加线上融资保扩张，保证了负债对投放业务的有效支撑，实现了资产规模的快速增长和市场份额的稳中有升。通过加大信贷资产和其他金融资产的交互投放，双动力驱动实现了营业收入的持续增长，避免了在严峻的利率市场化和不良贷款反弹的大环境中，经营效益的断崖式下滑，为“十二五”的收官画上了圆满的句号，也为本公司的战略转型“过冬”打下了第一步基础。

##### (3) 认真履行了风险防控责任

在2015年多重复杂的金融政策和经济形势下，本公司连续多年取得的风险防控成果面临了极大的挑战。从全面风险管理总体组织，到信用风险、关联交易、信息系统、股权管理、流动性风险、法治银行建设、反洗钱、消费者权益保护等多个具体工作中，董事会认真落实风险防控的最终责任，坚持风险报告审议制度，适时提出风险防范的重点。全年审议风险管理类议案或提出指导意见共6项，在风险管理战略方面提出了策略方针，在风险管理战术方面做出了具体部署，打胜了困难时期风险防控的战役。

一是保证了充裕的流动性，安全渡过了利率市场化的最后关键时刻；二是在特殊经济环境下，全国银行业不良资产普遍大反弹的形势下，及时定策略、抓重点，做到了信贷资产风险的完全可控；三是计提的损失准备金对全部不良贷款满足了1.5倍以上的全覆盖；四是实现了全年无案件、无重大操作事件、无重大信息系统事件。

##### (4) 积极推动了重要工作任务的实施

2015年，董事会积极推动，全面落实对本公司长远发展有重要意义的工作项目的落实，取得了良好的成果。

注册资本3亿元人民币的全省第一家消费金融公司正在积极筹建，筹建方案已上报中国银监会待批。年内全面完成了30亿元规模的小微金融债券的发行准备工作。出资20亿元的青海省内第一支轻工产业基金成功设立。信贷资产证券化发行资质进入实质性申报阶段，大额存单发行前期准备工作顺利启动。一系列负债和资产结构性优化工作的实施，为今后的增收创利打下了良好的基础。

本公司以新三板挂牌标准为尺度的公开募集股份前期准备工作在董事会的大力支持下全面启动，理清了本公司在股权沿革、财务指标、资产权属、依法合规经营情况和资产质量等方面存在的大量历史遗留问题，为本公司今后走向公开资本市场打下了良好的基础，也为提高内部管理工作水平起到了积极的作用。

## 九、董事会报告

##### (5) 进一步增强了企业的社会责任

本公司始终以实际行动践行国有企业的社会责任。2015年，在国内经济下行，实体经济效益普遍下滑的情况下，对实体企业坚持不抽贷、不断贷、不限贷，并多途径为困难企业排忧解难。全年办理工业企业贷款展期、借新还旧和重组金额达8.2亿元。并通过利率调整，为实体经济减轻融资负担，全年让利3000万元以上。

2015年，本公司在自身效益受到宏观经济严重影响的情况下，仍保持了纳税的相对稳定，全年上缴各项税收3.88亿元。本公司连续7年进入“青海企业50强”，连续第2年入选中国服务业企业500强，连续第3年被省政府授予工业经济运行突出贡献奖，被《银行家》杂志评为“2014年度全国资产规模500-1000亿元城市商业银行财务竞争力评价第一名”，被《当代金融家》杂志评为“2014年最佳中小银行最佳特色银行奖”，荣获“2014-2015年全国企业文化优秀成果奖”“2012-2013年度A级纳税信用单位”等荣誉称号。

#### 3、董事会对股东大会决议的执行情况

2015年4月24日，本公司2014年度股东大会审议通过了“按照2014年度审计后的净利润的10%提取法定盈余公积95,959,685.47元、计提一般风险准备93,308,586.56元，并以186,139万股股份为基数，每股派发现金0.12元，共计派发现金22,336.69万元”利润分配方案，本公司董事会认真落实股东大会决议，圆满完成上述法定盈余公积、一般风险准备金计提和股利派发工作。同时，本公司董事会认真组织经营管理层贯彻落实2015年度工作计划及财务预算方案，积极完成年度各项工作任务。

#### 4、2015年利润分配方案

经青海大正会计师事务所审计确认，2015年本公司实现净利润672,171,961.30元。根据有关法律法规和本公司章程的规定，2015年税后利润拟作以下分配：

(1) 按照2015年度审计后的净利润的10%提取法定盈余公积，共提取67,217,196.13元；

(2) 根据财政部有关规定，按2015年末本行风险资产余额测算，计提一般风险准备199,786,087.52元；

(3) 以1,861,391,038股股份为基数，每股派发现金0.10元，共计派发现金186,139,103.80元；

(4) 剩余利润留存以后年度分配。

#### 5、2016年董事会工作计划

2016年，在面临着多重困难的情况下，本公司将全力打好“改革创新”和“风险防控”两张牌，以党建引领企业发展方向，以改革创新推动新发展动力的提升，以风险防控为新发展动力保驾护航，继续以时间换取发展空间，稳定经营业绩，逐步走出新旧动力转换接续困难的时期。

#### 2016年主要经营目标：

(1) 实现净利润基本与2015年持平，达到6.6亿元；

(2) 不良贷款率控制在2%以下；

(3) 净资产收益率超过12%；

(4) 各项监管指标全部达标。

#### 董事会工作重点：

(1) 全力推动事关全局的重点改革发展工作

2016年，董事会要紧抓重点改革发展项目，以多点提升带动全面发展。

一是以优化资本结构为核心，推动资本管理战略。积极研究非公开发行、定向增发、资本债券等外源资本补充的可行性，引进对本公司发展战略有帮助、发展业务有支持的战略性投资机构，在确保资本对业务有效支撑和资本充足率达标的同时，推动本公司进一步优化股权结构，逐步健全有利于本公司发展的、适应市场要求的现代企业管理体制和经营机制，从根本上解决本公司的发展动力问题。

二是加快发展普惠金融、绿色金融，加大对经济社会发展薄弱环节的支持力度，让更多的实体经济和社会民众享受到便捷、高效、优质的金融服务。要紧抓全省经济结构性改革的重大机遇，增加对基础设施、产业升

## 九、董事会报告

级、民生改善、生态保护、科技创新项目的投放力度，支持去产能、去库存、去杠杆、降成本、补短板五项发展任务，坚决退出淘汰落后产能项目。

三是继续推动业务转型进程，增强发展新动力。在小微金融发展模式建设取得经验的基础上，将国家政策鼓励的消费金融和农业金融统一纳入大零售组织管理架构，加大转型力度，适应市场需求的变化。建立“大零售”管理与运营格局，以投贷联动为抓手，各前台业务条线要各司其职、同频共振、深入转型、释放活力。全力推动零售和小微业务资源共享、深度融合、联动营销、协调发展。2016年年初本公司已经成功发行同业存单，下半年开始发行大额存单，进一步拓宽负债渠道。积极推动资产证券化前期准备工作，重点关注不良资产证券化的政策性和可行性，适时跟进。严格落实消费金融公司筹建准入条件，力争年内开业，迈出本公司资本投资多元化发展第一步。做好小微金融债券发行管理工作，确保30亿元规模年内发行成功并满足相关监管要求。继续扩大基金式资产投资规模，压缩低收益资产配置占比，促进资产结构的优化调整，建立与负债结构相适应的、收益有保障的大资产管理模式。

四是利用国家和地方支持民族地区经济，发展金融的各项政策，继续下沉服务机构。力争黄南藏族自治州分行和农业经济较发达的门源、民和县域支行年内开业。

五是紧密结合即将完成的青海省“十三五”金融发展规划，科学规划本公司未来三至五年的发展战略，为本公司确定清晰的发展方向，可行的发展方式。

六是积极利用国家全面实施营改增的机会，认真筹划营改增方案，争取最大限度将税收政策转变为实际利益。

(2) 全力加强事关全局的风险防控工作

2016年，董事会要在总结以往风险防控经验的基础上，进一步强化全面风险管理体系建设，要充分发挥风险防控最终管理者的职责，加强督导落实各层级风险防控的具体责任，把握稳增长、调结构、增效益与防风险之间的平衡，继续实施稳健的风险偏好和风险管理策略，坚守风险防范的底线不突破。

一是重点关注受经济下行影响较大的实体企业贷款，加强对企业的贷后检查工作，对已经发生还本付息困难的企业要制订一户一策的解决方案，确保不良贷款的反弹在可控范围内不突破。

二是高度警惕外部各种风险因素向内部的传导，强化内部风险预警管理，弱化外部风险因素对业务发展的负面影响。

三是在负债规模快速扩张的同时，继续做好流动性风险管理工作，加强与人行的沟通协调，积极利用再贷款、常备借贷便利等货币政策工具，增强流动性风险防控能力。

四是继续加大损失准备金计提力度，全年计提损失准备金不低于2亿元，并逐步增加非信贷资产减值准备的计提。

##### (3) 全力完善以董事会为核心的公司治理结构

董事会将继续发挥在公司治理结构中的核心作用，认真领会中央和地方有关国有企业改革发展的指导意见，进一步明晰董事会的责权，将公司治理结构的完善作为长期工作持续推动。

一是要继续加强与相关领导机构的汇报沟通，力争早日完成四届董事会、监事会的换届工作。

二是根据青海省政府和国资委有关国有企业改革发展的要求，进一步强化董事会与党委的沟通协调机制，既充分发挥党委的政治核心作用，也充分落实董事会在重大决策、薪酬考核等方面的职权，形成内部协调运转、有效制衡的决策、执行、监督体系。

三是根据国资委对国有企业业绩考核制度的修改调整和建立企业职业经理人的要求，对原有的高管薪酬考核办法进行相应的调整完善，并积极探索建立与需求相结合的、与地方其他金融机构有可比性的本公司职业经理人管理体系。

# IX. Report by the Board of Directors

## 1. Review of overall business of 2015

In the year 2015, the Company’s main business indexes are completed as follows:

(1) Total asset of 70,435 million yuan, increasing by 17,101 million yuan compared with the last year, and increasing by 32.06%;

(2) Kinds of deposits 51,986 million yuan, increasing by 12,336 million yuan compared with the last year, and increasing by 31.11%;

(3) Kinds of loans 35,699 million yuan, increasing by 6,772 million yuan compared with the last year, and increasing by 23.41%;

(4) Kinds of revenue 3,847 million yuan, increasing by 605 million yuan, and increasing by 18.66%;

(5) Net profit is 672 million yuan, decreasing by 287 million yuan and decreasing by 29.95%;

(6) Net asset yield is 12.51%, decreasing by 7.89% compared with the last year;

(7) Year-end balance of non-performing loan is 670 million yuan, increasing by 399 million yuan compared with the end of the last year; and the ratio is 1.88%, increasing by 0.94% compared with the last year.

## 2. Review of work of the Board of Directors in 2015

### (1) Guarantee stability of governance structure of the Company

In the year 2015, with “managing key issues, clarifying direction and timely decision-making”, taken actual situations into account and in terms of system building, business exploration, risk management and control, organization setting and senior officers allocation etc., the Board of Directors of the Company fully plays its role with authorities authorized by the Shareholders’ Assembly. Throughout the year, the Board of Directors convoked 16 meetings of the Board of Directors, reviewed 16 proposals and took 6 reports. The professional commission convoked 25 meetings, reviewed 33 proposals and took 1 report to guarantee stable and reliable operation of corporate structure of the Company.

##### (2) Effectively yield the function of business guide

At the beginning of the year 2015, under extremely difficult economic situations, the Board of Directors analyzed internal and external development factors, adhered to the principle of business driven by reform and innovation and actualized improvement of sustainable business. Throughout the year, the interest rate pricing policy was used to stabilize financing as foundation, market orientation was used to add online financing to ensure expansion to guarantee effective support of application by liabilities, and quick increasing of asset scale and market share was achieved. Dual drive realized continuous growth of income by enhancing interaction of credit loan asset and other financial assets to avoid sharp falling in the environment of severe market-oriented interest rate and rebounding non-performing loan to complete the "Twelfth Five-year Plan" successfully, and lay a solid foundation for "passing the winter" in the Company's strategic transformation.

##### (3) Carefully exercise risk prevention and control responsibilities

Under the complicated financial policy and economic situations in 2015, the risk prevention and control achievements obtained by the Company met great challenges. From overall risk management and organization to credit risk, related trades, information system, equity management, liquidity risk, law-governance bank building, anti-money laundering and consumers' rights and interests protection, the Board of Directors carefully implemented final responsibilities of risk prevention and control, adhered to the risk report review system and proposed keys of risk prevention and control. The Board of Directors reviewed risk management proposals or guide opinions with 6 issues in total, proposed strategy guideline in terms of risk management strategy, made deployment in risk management strategy, and won risk prevention and control in tough period.

#### IX. Report by the Board of Directors

The first is to ensure sufficient liquidity and spend the final key moment of market-oriented interest rate; the second is to draft strategy, key issues and control risks of credit loan assets under special economic environment and rebounding of non-performing assets of bank industry in China; the third is to ensure that loss provision withdrawn covers 1.5 times of all non-performing loan and above; the fourth is to avoid incidents, key operation incidents and key information incidents throughout the year.

##### (4) Actively boost implementation of key assignment

In the year 2015, the Board of Directors actively boosted to implement programs significant for the Company's long-term development and made sound achievements.

The first consumption financial company in the province with registered capital of RMB 300 million yuan is under preparation, and the proposal has been submitted to the China Banking Regulatory Commission for approval. The preparation for issuing small and micro-enterprise financial bonds with scale of 3,000 million yuan was completed in the year. The first light industry fund in Qinghai Province with contribution of 2,000 million yuan was established. Issuing qualification of securitization of credit loan assets entered substantial declaration stage, and preparation for issuing large-amount deposit receipt was started successfully. Implementation of optimization of liability and asset structure laid a solid foundation for increasing income and creating benefits in the future.

Strongly supported by the Board of Directors, preparations for public offering according to the standard of the new three boards were completely launched to clear off a lot of leftover issues in terms of stock right evolution, financial indexes, asset ownership, conforming business according to laws and asset quality to lay a sound foundation for the Company's walking into open market and play an active role in improving internal management.

##### (5) Further reinforce corporate social responsibilities

The Company exercised social responsibilities of state-owned enterprise with practice. In the year 2015, under situations of downward domestic economic conditions and dropping benefits of entity economy, the Company adhered to not drawing loan, suspending load and restricting loan for entity enterprises, and removes difficulties and troubles for enterprises in trouble. Throughout the year, the Company handled load term extension, repaying the old debt by the new loan and reconstruction with total amount of 820 million yuan. The Company relieved financing burden of entity economy by adjusting interest rate, and surrendered profit of more than 30 million yuan in the year.

In the year 2015, under the situations that the Company's profits were affected by macroscopic economy, the Company kept reliably stable tax, and paid tax of 388 million yuan in the year. The Company was listed "Top 50 Enterprises of Qinghai Province" for consecutive 7 years, the top 500 enterprises of service industry of China for consecutive 2 years, was awarded of outstanding contribution for industrial and economic operation by the provincial government for consecutive 3 years, "No.1 for Financial Competitive Strength of Urban Commercial Bank with Asset Scale of 50,000 Million to 100,000 Million yuan in China in 2014" by the magazine the Banker, the Best Medium and Small Banks in 2014-the Best Featuring Bank" by the magazine Modern Banker, and "Level-A Credit Unit for Tax Payment from 2012 to 2013" by Qinghai State Tax Bureau and Local Tax Bureau.

## 3. Implementation of resolution adopted by Shareholders' Conference by the Board of Directors

On April 24, 2015, the Shareholders' Conference of the Company reviewed and passed the profit distribution plan "statutory surplus public reserve of 95,959,685.47 yuan is withdrawn based on the audited profit for the year 2014, the general risk provision 93,308,586.56 yuan is withdrawn, based on 1,861.39 million shares as base, and cash of

# IX. Report by the Board of Directors

0.12 yuan is distributed for each share, for a total payment of cash 223.3669 million yuan". The Board of Directors of the Company implemented resolutions adopted by the Board of Directors, and finished the statutory surplus public reserve, general risk provision withdrawal and dividend distribution. Meanwhile, the Board of Directors of the Company implemented the work plan and financial budget for the year 2015, and finished assignment for the year 2015.

## 4. Profit distribution proposal for year 2015

Audited and confirmed by Qinghai Dazheng Accounting Firm, net profit realized by the Company in 2015 is 672,171,961.30 yuan. According to relevant laws and Articles of Association of the Company, the after-tax profit for the year 2015 is distributed as follows:

(1) 10% of the audited profit for year 2015 is withdrawn as statutory surplus public reserve, and 67,217,196.13 yuan is withdrawn;

(2) According to relevant provisions of the Ministry of Finance, based on balance of risk assets of the Bank at the end of the year 2015, the general risk provision 199,786,087.52 yuan is withdrawn;

(3) With 1,861,391,038 shares as base, cash of 0.10 yuan is distributed for each share, and cash of 186,139,103.80 yuan is distributed;

(4) The remaining profit retention will be distributed in the following years.

## 5. Work plan of the Board of Directors in 2016

In the year 2016, under difficulties, the Company will operate “reform innovation” and “risk prevention and control”, lead development of the enterprise with Party construction, boost new development with reform and innovation, safeguard the new development with risk prevention and control, continue to exchange development space with time, stabilize business performance and walk out of the shifting period of new and old dynamics.

Main business objectives of the year 2016:

(1) Net profit almost the same with that of the year 2015, and reaching 660 million yuan;

(2) Non-performing loan rate is controlled below 2%;

(3) Net rate of return of asset is more than 12%;

(4) All supervision indexes satisfy requirements.

##### Key work of the Board of Directors:

##### (1) Advance key development and reform related to overall conditions

In the year 2016, the Board of Directors will seize the key development and reform and drive overall development with multi-point improvement.

Firstly, center optimizing capital structure, and advanced capital management strategy. Actively research feasibility of non-public offering, private placement, capital debenture and other external sources, introduce strategic investment body beneficial for the Company's corporate strategy and supporting the Company's business, while ensuring effective support of business and capital sufficiency ratio, further advance optimization of equity structure of the Company, establish and perfect modern enterprise management system and business system beneficial for corporate development and adaptable to the requirements, and solve the Company's development dynamics fundamentally.

Secondly, accelerate development of popularized finance and ecofriendly finance, increase support for weak procedures of economic and social development, and make more entity economy and social receive convenient,

# IX. Report by the Board of Directors

convenient and high-quality financial services. Seize the opportunities of structural reform of the Province, increase issuing scale of infrastructure, industry upgrading, livelihood improvement, ecological protection, science and technology innovation, support development of de-capacity, de-stocking, de-leverage, cost lowering and removing disadvantages, and remove the projects with outdated production capacity.

Thirdly, actively advance business transformation process, and increase the dynamics for development. Based on acquiring experience for financial development of pretty and micro-finance, take consumption finance encouraged by the state and the agriculture finance into the great retail organization structure, increase transformation strength and adapt the changes of market demands. Establish the "great retail" management and operation layout, on the basis of linking investment and loan, and the business staff shall exercise duties, work closely, transforms and release vigor. Advance sharing resources of retail and pretty and micro-business, in-depth combination, linking marketing, and coordinated development. At the beginning of the year 2016, the Bank issued inter-bank deposit, and will issue large-amount deposit in the second half of the year to expand debt financing channels. Actively prepare for securitization of assets, focus on policy and feasibility of non-performing assets, and follow up closely. Closely implement the preparation and access conditions for consumption and financial companies, try to open business in the year, and march towards the first step for divers step of the Company's capital. Run issuing and management of micro- and pretty business, ensure success issuing of 3,000 million yuan which satisfies the supervision requirements. Continue to increase fund-based investment scale, compress low-proceeds asset ratio, promote optimization and adjustment of asset structure, and establish the large-asset management mode with corresponding debt structure and guaranteed proceeds.

Fourthly, with the policy of supporting regional economy and developing finance by the state and local governments, continue to develop root business. The Huangnan Tibet Autonomous Prefecture Branch and Menyuan and Minhe Sub-branch with developed agricultural economy will be opened in this year.

Fifthly, combined with the financial development plan in the “Thirteenth Five-year Plan” in Qinghai Province, scientifically plan development strategy of the Company in the following 3 to 5 years, determine clear and feasible development trend of the Company.

Sixthly, with the opportunity of business tax changed into value-added tax, implement the proposal of business tax changed into value-added tax, and change the tax policy into actual interests.

##### (2) Fully reinforce risk control in overall situations

In the year 2016, based on summarizing previous risk control experience, the Board of Directors further reinforces construction of overall risk control, fully plays duties of the ultimate risk control managers, reinforces supervision and implementation of risk prevention and control, balances among growth, structure regulating, increasing benefit and risk prevention, continue to implement the stable risk prevention and risk management strategy, and safeguard the baseline of risk control.

Firstly, focus on the loan issued to entity enterprises significantly affected by economic downturn, reinforce post-lending inspection for enterprises, draft solutions for enterprises difficult in repaying principal and paying interest case by case, and ensure that rebounding of non-controlling loan is under control.

Secondly, alert transmission of various external factors from outside to inside, reinforce internal risk alert management, and weaken negative influences on business development by external risk factors.

# IX. Report by the Board of Directors

Thirdly, while liabilities are expanded, continue to manage liquidity risk management, reinforce communicating and coordinating with the Central Bank, and reinforce liquidity risk prevent and control with re-loan, standing lending facility and other currency policy instruments.

Fourthly, continue to increase loss provision withdrawal strength, the loss withdrawn provision in the year is not lower than 200 million yuan, and increase withdrawal of depreciation provision of non-credit loan assets.

##### (3) Perfect the corporate governance structure centering the Board of Directors

The Board of Directors will continue to play the core function in corporate governance structure, carefully understand the guide opinions for reform and development of state-owned enterprises by the central and local governments, further clarify duties and powers of the Board of Directors, and advance perfection of corporate governance structure as a long-term task.

Firstly, continue to reinforce reporting to and communicating with competent leaders, and try to complete reelection of the 4th session of Board of Directors and Board of Supervisor.

Secondly, according to requirements on reform and development of state-owned enterprises by government and the State-owned Asset Commission of Qinghai Province, further reinforce communicating and coordinating mechanism between the Board of Directors and the Party Committee, not only playing political core of the Party Committee, but also implementing powers of key decision making and remuneration assessment by the Board of Directors, and form internationally coordinating and operating and effectively balancing decision making, execution and supervision systems.

Thirdly, according to requirements on modification and adjustment on performance assessment on state-owned enterprises and establishment of occupational managers by the State-owned Asset Commission, the former remuneration assessment for senior officers is adjusted and perfected, and the occupational manager system for the Company taken into the Company's situations into account and comparable with other local financial institutions shall be established.

2015年，青海银行切实履行社会责任，支持青海省经济建设和慈善事业发展，向青海省红十字会捐款100万元。同时，积极组织员工参与“走进福利院，关爱老年人”等多项公益活动。

In the year 2015, the Bank of Qinghai exercised social duties, supported economic construction and charity undertaking of Qinghai City and donated 1 million yuan to the Red-cross Society of Qinghai Province. Meanwhile, the Bank organized staff to participate in public welfare programs such as “walk into the charity house and care the aged”.

## 十、监事会报告

### 1、2015年监事会工作情况

(1) 持续强化对风险管控工作的监督。根据本公司风险现状，重点加强对信用风险、流动性风险、操作风险和声誉风险的关注和监督力度，进一步提高全行风险防控水平。同时，紧紧围绕2015年本公司中心工作，坚持“风险防控为本、稳健经营为上”的全面风险管理理念，立足全面风险管控、服务本公司快速稳健发展的宗旨，不断强化重点领域风险防控工作，持续关注政府平台、房地产、高载能企业贷款风险及风险抵补准备；加强案件防控和流动性风险管理工作的监督，密切关注负面舆情影响与化解工作，促进全行安全稳健发展。

(2) 加强对财务管理工作的检查。密切关注和监督影响财务收益的各项指标的执行情况，重点监督检查经营层对董事会核批的财务预算执行情况；监督检查各分支机构对总行下达财务收支计划执行情况及核拨费用的使用与管理情况；监督检查账务核算与年度利润的真实性；对重大项目支出实施专项监督检查，并提出改进提升财务管理的意见和建议。

(3) 全面强化内部审计工作。2015年，监事会会同董事会指导内部审计部门在抓好全行业务薄弱环节开展常规检查的基础上，及时创新审计思路，消除审计空白区域，在全辖首次开展了信贷业务尽职调查审计、小企业金融服务中心信贷新产品专项审计、消费者权益保护专项审计、绩效考核专项审计和全面内控评价，聘请北京时代新威信息技术有限公司开展信息科技全面风险外部审计。同时，委托审计部对全辖各分支行及相关业务部门2015年上半年审计、合规、会计、信贷、零售等条线检查发现的问题后续整改落实情况，以及2015年上半年“两个加强、两个遏制”检查工作落实整改情况进行“回头看”专项检查。持续建立健全违规问责机制，加大问题整改落实力度，确保内部审计工作取得显著成效。全年对全辖各分支行及相关业务部门深入开展了29项常规审计、专项审计及特色审计。

(4) 持续推进监事会自身建设。将监事会工作融入到日常经营管理工作中，充分利用和发挥内审部门、外部审计机构和监管部门的监督检查成果，推进持续跟踪整改落实。进一步完善工作评价报告制度，密切加强与本公司董事会、经营管理层及监管部门的工作沟通与协调，全力支持和配合董事会、经营层的工作，共同促进本公司持续稳健发展。

2015年，监事会以现场和通讯方式共组织召开了9次监事会会议，审议了涉及年度报告、财务预决算方案、利润分配草案以及监事会相关工作报告等18项议案，听取重大报告6项。全体监事能够认真履行职责，会前认真研阅会议议题，会中认真审议每项议题并充分发表意见，会后关注议案的实施落地情况，切实履行了职责。

(5) 加强对董事、高级管理人员履职行为的考察。充分发挥监事会“观察员”和“监督员”作用，全面考核董事诚实守信、勤勉尽职、合规履职方面的表现；考核高级管理人员在业务技能、处理复杂问题以及长期承诺和责任心方面的表现。重点监督董事在决策和风险管控方面的履职行为；监督高级管理人员在推进业务转型和精细化管理执行力方面的履职行为。完善对董事、高级管理人员履职情况的评价报告制度，多角度对董事和高级管理人员履职情况进行考察和监督。

### 2、监事会就有关事项发表的独立意见

报告期内，根据本公司章程及中国银监会相关要求，本公司监事会通过与董事、高管人员访谈并列席董事会议、高管层经营会议及日常工作观察，按照有关股份制商业银行董事、高管人员履职尽职要求，对董事、高管人员2015年度履职情况作出如下评价意见：

(1) 监事会认为：2015年，董事会能够按照规定认真履职、勤勉尽责，积极贯彻执行国家金融方针政策和股东大会决议；高度重视公司治理和内控管理，自觉接受监事会的监督；准确研判内外形势，正确把握发展方向，科学制定战略决策，引领和推动了全行业务经营和改革创新。董事会成员能够以维护股东权益和推进本公司稳健发展为目标，以负责、审慎、科学的态度对年度内提交的涉及到公司机构发展、资本管理以及人力资源等多方

## 十、监事会报告

面问题进行深度思考，对资本融资、设立机构、聘任高级管理人员等多项议题充分发表意见，有效发挥了作为董事的决策作用，切实维护了全体股东的各项权益，对本公司的发展战略决策和风险管理较好地履行了董事应尽之责。

(2) 监事会认为：2015年，本公司高级管理人员能够认真贯彻执行监管机构对本公司的监管要求和董事会、监事会决议，在复杂严峻的经济金融形势下，着力提升战略发展能力、市场竞争能力、管理保障能力与和谐发展能力。在严峻的形势中逆势而上，经营业绩有了新的收获，转型发展实现新的突破，业务规模有了新的增长，风险管控实现新的提升，核心竞争力进一步提升，企业形象进一步凸显。



<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_505_360_1077_762.jpg" alt="Image" width="48%" /></div>


### 3、2016年监事会工作重点

(1) 加强对风险管控工作的监督。按照“比以往任何时候更加重视风险防控”的总体要求，密切关注并监督关乎本公司发展的风险控制状况，绷紧风险管控这根弦，监督董事会和经营层在“青海银行风险防控提升年”对重点风险防控的政策、程序和措施的健全与提升情况。重点监督经营层在整个经营过程中风险防控措施的落实执行情况。突出审慎管理理念，进一步强化对案件防控、流动性风险和业务连续性工作的监督，促进全行安全稳健运营。



(2) 加强对董事、高级管理人员履职情况的考核监督。进一步改进对董事、经营层高管人员履职情况的评价报告制度，多角度观察、监督、评价履职情况，提出监督评价意见和工作改进建议。加强与董事、高级管理人员的充分沟通，全力支持董事会和经营层开展工作，积极为全行工作建言献策，共同推动本公司经营管理水平再上新台阶。

(3) 加强对重大、重要政策实施落实情况的监督。对董事会制定的发展战略和风险控制策略的科学性、合理性和有效性进行评估。重点关注新三板挂牌上市等资本补充工作、消费金融公司挂牌运营等重大战略事项的推进情况。关注经营管理工作，推进业务转型和精细化管理，重点考察中小微金融业务、个人金融业务转型政策的落地实施情况、绩效管理政策的落实及其效应情况以及信息科技治理、安全运行、维护管理情况。

(4) 持续加强监事会监督效能。发挥求真务实精神，把监事会工作与本公司日常经营管理工作紧密结合起来，以强化自我约束机制为切入口，以组织专项审计、检查、调查、跟踪检查等手段，不断改善工作方法，提高工作实效。

(5) 持续加强监事会自身建设。找准新常态下监事会工作的落脚点。根据国资委及监管部门对公司治理体系的新要求，进一步完善监事会组织架构，明确监事会职责权限，建立健全各项监督管理制度，对监事履职情况进行考核评价。进一步创新监督管理手段上，探索建立和实施监事会调研巡查与质询工作机制，在组织开展现场审计工作的同时，组织监事会同步开展专项和综合性调研活动以及必要的质询工作。加强与监管部门的沟通联系，认真听取监管意见，努力构建一个积极的、进步的、创造价值的监事会。

## X. Report by the Board of Supervisors

### 1. Work of the Board of Supervisors in 2015

(1) Continue to reinforce supervision over risk management and control. According to current risk conditions of the Company, reinforce concern and supervision strength of credit risks, liquidity risks, operation risks and reputation risks, and further improve risk prevention and control of the Bank. Meanwhile, based on central work of the Company in 2015, the Company adhered to the overall risk management philosophy of "risk prevention and control centered, stable and sound operation foremost", aimed to keep the foothold of overall risk management and control, serve rapid and stable development of the Company, reinforced risk prevention and control of key fields and focused on loan risks and risk covering provision related to government platform, real estate and enterprises with high energy consumption; reinforced supervision over case prevention and control, and liquidity risk management, focused on negative public sentiment influences and solving, and promoted safe, sound and stable development of the Bank.

(2) Reinforce inspection over financial management. Closely focuses on and supervise execution of indexes having impact on financial benefits, and supervise the operation tier's execution of financial budget approved by the Board of Directors; supervise the branches' execution of financial income and expenses approved by the head office and use and management of verified and appropriated expenses; supervise, check and verify authenticity of annual profit; perform special supervision and inspection over significant expenses, and propose opinions and suggestions for financial management.

(3) Reinforce internal audit. In the year 2015, based on conducting regular inspection over weak business procedures of the Bank by the internal audit department, the Board of Directors and the Board of Supervisors brought forward the audit philosophy, eliminated gap of the audit, and carried out due diligence audit of credit loan business, special audit of new credit loan product of the small-enterprise financial service center, special audit of consumers' rights and interests protection, special audit of performance assessment and overall internal control assessment for the first time, and engaged Beijing Shidai Xinwei Information Technology Co., Ltd. in carrying out external audit of overall risks of information science and technology. Meanwhile, the Audit Department was consigned to check implement correction of any found issues in audit, conformity, accounting, credit loan and retail in the first half of the year 2015 by branches and involving business department, and exercised "looking back" check for correction of the "two reinforcements, two inhibitions" in the first half of the year 2015. Continued to establish and perfect the breaching accountability system, increase correct implementation strength of issues and ensure that internal audit achieves success. Performed 29 regular audits, special audits and featuring audits for branches, sub-branches and competent departments of the Bank.

(4) Continue to build the Board of Supervisors. Combine work of the Board of Directors into daily operation and management, and advance correction implementation with the supervision and inspection achievements by the internal audit department, external audit body and supervision authorities. Further perfect work assessment and reporting system, reinforce work communication and coordination with the Board of Directors, business management and supervision authorities of the Company, fully support and coordinate with work of the Board of Directors and the operation tier, and promote sustainable, sound and healthy development of the Company.

In the year 2015, the Board of Supervisors organized 9 meetings of the Board of Supervisors in field and telecommunication, reviewed 18 proposals including annual report, financial budget and final settlement proposal, profit distribution draft and work report of the Board of Supervisors, and took 6 key reports. All supervisors exercised duties, read proposals before meeting and released opinions, focused on implementation of proposals, and exercised duties.

# X. Report by the Board of Supervisors

(5) Reinforce inspection over performance of directors and senior officers. Fully yield the function of the Board of Supervisors as “observer” and “supervisor”, and fully assess performance of directors in terms of faith, diligence and conformity; assess performance of senior officers in terms of business skill, tackling complicated issues, long-term commitment and responsibility. Specially supervise performance of directors in terms of decision-making and risk management and control; supervise performance of senior officers in terms of advancing business transformation and fine management. Perfect assessment and reporting system in terms of performance of directors and senior officers, and inspect and supervise performance of directors and senior officers.

### 2. Independent opinions on pertinent issued by the Board of Supervisors

During the reporting period, according to requirements of the Articles of Association of the Company and by China Banking Regulatory Commission, by interviewing directors and senior officers, attending meetings of the Board of Directors and business conference of senior officers and daily work observation and in accordance with performance diligence requirements on directors and senior officers of stock commercial banks, the Board of Supervisors assesses performance of directors and senior officers in the year 2015 as follows:

(1) The Board of Supervisors holds the opinion that: in the year 2015, the Board of Directors exercised duties in due diligence, and actively implemented the national financial policy and resolutions adopted by the shareholders' conference; attached great importance to corporate governance and internal control management, and received supervision by the Board of Directors; accurately researched and judged internal and external situations, correctly controlled the trend of development, scientifically drafted strategic decision making, and led and advanced business operation and reform innovation of the Bank. Members of the Board of Directors aimed to maintain shareholders' equity and advance the Company's stable and sound development, reflected about issues related to the Company's organization development, capital management and human resources submitted in the year in a responsible, prudent and scientific attitude, issued opinions on capital financing, organization establishment, engaging senior officers and other proposals, effectively yielded equity of all shareholders, and exercised duties for the Company's development strategy decision-making and risk management.

(2) The Board of Supervisors thinks that: in the year 2015, senior officers of the Company exercises supervision requirements on the Company by the supervision authorities and resolutions of the Board of Directors and the Board of Supervisors, and improves strategic development capability, market competition capability, management guarantee capability and harmonic development capability under complicated and severe economic and financial conditions. The Company made efforts under severe conditions, achieved success in business performance, realized new breakthroughs in transformation development, increased business scale, improved risk management and control, improved core competitiveness strength and represented corporate image.

### 3. Key work of the Board of Supervisors in 2016

(1) Reinforce supervision over risk management and control. According to the general requirements “attaching more importance to risk prevention and control than ever”, closely focus and supervise risk control related to the Company’s development, well manage risk management and control, and supervise perfection and improvement of policy, procedure and measure of key risk prevention and control for “the Risk Prevention and Control Year of the Bank of Qinghai” by the Board of Directors and the business tier. Specially supervise implementation of risk prevention and control measures by the business tier. Highlight the philosophy of prudence management, further reinforce supervision over incident prevention and control, liquidity risks and business continuity, and promote safe, sound and healthy development.

#### X. Report by the Board of Supervisors

(2) Reinforce assessment on and supervision over performance of directors and senior officers. Further improve assessment report system for directors and senior officers of business tier, observe, supervise and assess performance in multiple dimensions, and propose supervision and assessment opinions and work improvement opinions. Reinforce sufficient communication with directors and senior officers, fully support work by the Board of Directors and business tier, provide suggestions for work of the Bank, and advance operation and management of the Company to a new level.

(3) Reinforce supervision over implementation of key policy. Assess scientific nature, rationality and effectiveness of development strategy and risk control strategy drafted by the Board of Directors. Focus on capital supplementing for listing in the "New Three Boards" and operation of the consumption financial company. Focus on business and management, advance business transformation and fine management, investigate medium, small and micro-financial business, implementation of individual financial business policy, implementation of performance management policy and its influences and information science& technology control, safe operation, maintenance and management.

(4) Continue to reinforce supervision function of the Board of Supervisors. Yield the spirit of realistic approach, closely combine work of the Board of Supervisors and the Company's daily operation and management, start with reinforcing self-discipline, improve work method and increase operation efficiency by means of organizing special audit, inspection, investigation and tracking inspection etc.

(5) Continue to reinforce building of the Board of Supervisors. Find out the stance of work of the Board of Supervisors under the new normal. According to new requirements on corporate governance by the State-owned Asset Commission and the supervision authorities, further perfect organization structure of the Board of Supervisors, clarify duties and authorities of the Board of Supervisors, establish and perfect supervision and management systems, and assess performance of the supervisors. In terms of further bringing forward supervision and management means, explore, establish and implement investigation, tour inspection and inquiry mechanism by the Board of Supervisors. While carrying out in-field audit, organize the Board of Supervisors to carry out special and comprehensive investigation and research activities and necessary inquiry. Reinforce communicating and liaison with the supervision authorities, take the supervision opinions, and try to build an active, enterprising and value-creating Board of Supervisors.

## 十一、重要事项

### 1、重大诉讼、仲裁事项

报告期内，本公司未发生对经营产生重大影响的诉讼、仲裁事项。

### 2、收购及出售资产、吸收合并事项

报告期内，本公司未发生重大收购及出售、吸收合并事项。

### 3、关联方关系及其交易情况

青海银行自成立以来，积极推进特色企业文化建设，坚持以优秀的文化和有效的人本理念凝心聚力，独具特色的企业核心价值观和团队观已深植于业务经营的各个层面，先进集体和优秀个人层出不穷，提高生产力的同时也为青海银行赢得了美誉和尊重。2015年，青海银行荣获中国企业联合会和中国企业家协会颁发的全国企业文化建设领域最高的奖项“2014-2015年全国企业文化优秀成果奖”。图为青海银行员工拾金不昧受客户赞扬。

Since foundation, the Bank of Qinghai actively advances the featuring corporate culture building and adheres to root excellent culture and valid human-centered philosophy cohesion, featuring corporate core values and team value into each and every tier of business, giving birth to many advanced collectives and excellent individuals, and winning reputation and respect for the Bank of Qinghai while productive force has been improved. In the year 2015, the Bank of Qinghai was awarded to the top ward "Achievement Award for National Corporate Culture from 2014 to 2015" in national corporate culture building issued by China Enterprise Confederation and China Enterprise Director Association. The Figure shows that the staff of the Bank of Qinghai was praised for returning lost money.

## 十二、财务报告

本公司2015年度财务报告经青海大正会计师事务所有限公司注册会计师吴海泉、李勇根据中国注册会计师独立审计准则，实施了审计工作，审计报告和经审计确认后的财务会计报表如下：

### 审计报告

#### 青海银行股份有限公司全体股东：

我们审计了后附的青海银行股份有限公司(以下简称贵公司)财务报表，包括2015年12月31日的资产负债表，2015年度的利润表、现金流量表和股东权益变动表以及财务报表附注。

## 一、管理层对财务报表的责任

编制和公允列报财务报表是贵公司管理层的责任。这种责任包括：(1)按照企业会计准则的规定编制财务报表，并使其实现公允反映；(2)设计、执行和维护必要的内部控制，以使财务报表不存在由于舞弊或错误而导致的重大错报。

## 二、注册会计师责任

我们的责任是在执行审计工作的基础上对财务报表发表审计意见。我们按照中国注册会计师审计准则的规定计划执行了审计工作。中国注册会计师审计准则要求我们遵守中国注册会计师职业道德守则，计划和执行审计工作以对财务报表是否存在重大错报获取合理保证。

审计工作涉及实施审计程序，以获取有关财务报表金额和披露的审计证据。选择的审计程序取决于注册会计师的判断，包括对由于舞弊或错误导致的财务报表重大错报风险的评估。在进行风险评估时，注册会计师考虑与财务报表编制和公允列报相关的内部控制，以设计恰当的审计程序，但目的并非对内部控制的有效性发表意见。审计工作还包括评价管理层选用会计政策的恰当性和作出会计估计的合理性，以及评价财务报表的总体列报。

我们相信，我们获取的审计证据是充分、适当的，为发表审计意见提供了基础。

## 三、审计意见

我们认为，青海银行股份有限公司财务报表在所有重大方面按照企业会计准则的规定编制，公允反映了贵公司2015年12月31日的财务状况以及2015年度的经营成果和现金流量。

# XII. Financial Report

The Company's financial report of year 2015 has been audited by the CPA Wu Haiquan and Liyong of Qinghai Dazheng Account Firm Co., Ltd. in accordance with the independent audit code of CPA of China, and the audit report and the audited and confirmed financial accounting statement are given as follows:

### Audit Report

#### To all shareholders of Bank of Qinghai Co., Ltd.

We have audited the attached financial report forms of Bank of Qinghai Co., Ltd. (hereafter referred to as the Company), including balance sheet on December 31, 2015, profit sheet, statement of change in shareholder equity, cash flow sheet and annotation for financial reporting for 2015.

### I. Responsibility for accounting reporting forms by the management

Preparation and fair disclosure of financial statement are your Company management's responsibilities, which include (1) prepare financial statements in accordance with the corporate accounting code to be reflected fairly; (2) design, execute and maintain necessary internal control to make the financial statement free of any significant errors due to embezzlement or errors.

### II. CPA's responsibility

Our responsibility is to make comment based on auditing the reporting form. We have performed according to auditing principle for China's CPA, which requires us to observe vocational and moral standard, plan and perform audit and without key mistake.

Audit involves audit proof and audited amount and disclosed audit proof. The selected audit procedure depends on CPA's judge, including assessment on possible key mistake by embezzlement or mistake. When assessing risk, CPA will consider relevant internal control related to compilation of the reporting form and appropriate audit procedure, but not to make comment on validity of internal control. Audit also includes correction of accounting policy and reasonability of accounting estimate and comment on the whole reporting forms.

We believe that the audit proof obtained by us is sufficient and reasonable and paves the way for making audit comment.

### III. Audit comment

We think, the financial statements of the Bank of Qinghai in all significant aspects in accordance with the Accounting Standards for Enterprise has been compiling the provisions, fairly reflects financial situations of your company dated on December 31, 2015, business achievement and cash flow of the year 2015.

Qinghai Dazheng Certified Public Accountants Co., Ltd.
